Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in biophysics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 211 and the invariant I(A,B) = 308 mod 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the biophysics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 211 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 308. QED.
